\section*{Check of the author's edited file (2026-11-22): summary}
\noindent\textbf{Question of the author:} ``Is this correct?''\quad \textbf{Answer: not yet.} The equations that the author kept are correct (every labelled equation was compared with the verified file \texttt{Final\_terms\_explicit.tex}, script \texttt{scripts/s11\_author1\_checks.py}); the differences are \emph{equivalent rewritings} except for the items below, which have to be corrected before the file can be used as the result. Nothing in the file was changed; the NOTE boxes are at the places concerned.
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Error (typesetting that changes the term)}, Group I Row III, last term (Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3}, Sec.~5): the recoil factor is written $e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$, i.e.\ $\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$ \emph{outside} the exponent. Correct: $e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}$ (as in Sec.~6, where it is right). As typeset the term is a different one (difference up to $2.9$ in units of the kernel, check~(4)).
\item \textbf{Prescription of Group I Row III (resolved):} the $i\epsilon$ term $\Delta N$ was removed; by the author's decision of 2026-11-22 the row is given with the principal value only, so this is correct. The conventions should say so, and the averaged $\Delta N$ rows in the commented-out Sec.~7 (Eqs.~(E-I.IIIdN-3), (E-I.IIIdN-2)) should be deleted.
\item \textbf{Undefined symbol:} Group I Row V, second part (Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I5two}) is written with $\xi$, which is not defined anywhere in the file (the definition $\xi\equiv p^{+}/k^{+}$ was in the deleted text; $\xi$ also denotes the DGLAP fraction in Sec.~3). With $\xi=p^{+}/k^{+}$ it is identical to the $p^{+}$ form used in Sec.~6 (check (3): $4\times10^{-16}$; with $\xi=1-p^{+}/k^{+}$ it would be wrong). Either define $\xi$ or use the $p^{+}$ form (the author's request of 2026-11-22).
\item \textbf{Symmetrisation identity}, Eq.~\eqref{eq:D-ord}: (a) the third contact term carries a second factor $\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}$ inside the bracket opened by $\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}\big[$; correct: $\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}\big[f^{abr}\delta_{12}\{\rho^r\rho^c\}+f^{bcr}\delta_{23}\{\rho^a\rho^r\}+f^{acr}\delta_{13}\{\rho^r\rho^b\}\big]$ (this typo is also in our verified file; the identity as intended is checked at the operator level to $4\times10^{-16}$, check (2); all rows of Sec.~6 use the correct form); (b) the subscript ${}_{\rm (iii)}$ on $\rho^{s}(p_1)$ is a leftover of the deleted \texttt{\textbackslash underbrace}; (c) ``ordered product of three Wilson lines'' should read ``ordered product of three colour charges $\rho$''.
\item \textbf{Silently omitted terms} (correct after the average, not at the operator level): the one-$\rho$ (double-commutator) terms (iii) of Eqs.~\eqref{eq:D-ord}--\eqref{eq:D-r21} are not written in any row of Sec.~6 and the file does not say so. They vanish after the average over a colour-invariant projectile ($\langle\rho^{s}\rangle=0$); Sec.~6 as a statement about the operators is complete only with them. Add one sentence.
\item \textbf{Conventions deleted:} the definition of the plus prescriptions (acting on the whole $p^{+}$-dependent function, recoil phase and energy denominators; Eq.~(plus) of the verified file), the meaning of $\int_{0}^{\infty}dp^{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')p^{+}/k^{+}}=k^{+}[\pi\delta(s)-i\mathcal P\frac1s]$ (instantaneous terms; the two terms of the bracket under one $y'$ integral), $\vee\to\infty$ before $\epsilon\to0$, PV/$i\epsilon$, pure glue ($N_f=0$), and the order of integrations are no longer in the file. The formulas cannot be evaluated unambiguously without them (e.g.\ $[\frac{1}{p^{+}}]_{+}$ acting on $e^{\dots p^{+}/k^{+}}$ only, or with the energy denominators). Restore the conventions section.
\item \textbf{Information on the tail-only cancellations deleted} (no term dropped): all terms of the soft-remainder pairs A--D, the 24-term kernel with $(\ell-\mathcal E)$ of Row III and the three rows of the joint tail (Group II Rows I, IV; Group III Row III) are still present in Secs.~5 and 6 --- correct. But the statements that they are integrable only in these combinations (pairs under one $z$ integral with $y'\to w'$, pair D also $x\leftrightarrow y$; the $w$ integral before the $z$ integral in Row III; the joint integration over the gluon-pair position) were in the deleted Sec.~C and must go with the result.
\item \textbf{Sec.~7 (average) is commented out} except the two averaging formulas: lines 608--945 (\texttt{\textbackslash begin\{comment\}}\dots\texttt{\textbackslash end\{comment\}}: all Group/Row singlet equations, including $\Delta N$, Group II Row IV with its remnant, Group III Rows II--VI) are not compiled, so the PDF contains no averaged result. The commented equations are identical to the verified ones. In the second averaging formula the opening $\langle$ is missing (correct: $\langle d^{efg}\{\rho^e\rho^f\rho^g\}_{\rm sym}\rangle$); the coefficient $\frac{N_c}{(N_c^2-4)(N_c^2-1)}$ is correct (check (5)).
\item \textbf{UV (author's additional question):} no term of Sec.~6 has a UV divergence and none has a $1/\epsilon$ pole; every term is absolutely integrable at every coincidence of its integration points, except the collinear point $z\to x$ of the two-$\rho$ term of Group I Row V (second part), which is integrable only with a rotation-invariant (angular-averaged) treatment around $z=x$ and only together with its $\frac{11}{6}$ term. Table and method: Sec.~\ref{sec:chk-UV}.
\end{enumerate}
\textbf{Correct (checked):} BFKL, JIMWLK, DGLAP, RC sections (only the red phase markers removed); Group I Rows II, IV, V; Group II Rows I, IV (the remnant term is now included, merged into Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-II4rem} --- correct); Group III Rows I, II, III, V, VI in Sec.~5; in Sec.~6 every row equals the verified one after substitution of the colour tensors $C^{abc}_{\rm row}$ (moving $f^{gde}$, $f^{mkl}$, $f^{mnk}$ into the prefactor and writing $+{\rm c.c.}$ explicitly is equivalent); Group I Row IV and Group III Row I are listed as cancelled (exact, verified). The file compiles (pdflatex, twice, no errors, no undefined references; 3 overfull boxes).
}

\section{BFKL contribution}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log\Big[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\Big]\,\int_{x,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\Bigg[ \notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(x')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(x')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(w-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,N_{c}\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{a}(x') -\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& -\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') +\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& +\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') +\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x') +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(z)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^{2}}\Big[\notag\\
& -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x') +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\notag\\
& +N_{c}\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x') -N_{c}\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(z)\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
In color singlet form, we get
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{1}{N^{2}_{c}-1}\,\log\Big[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\Big]\,\int_{x,y,z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\langle\rho^{f}(x)\rho^{f}(y) \rangle\,\Bigg[ \notag\\ & \mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,x,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+K(w,w',z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w')\cdot\mathcal K(w-z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(x)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w')\cdot\mathcal K(w-z)\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(x)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(w'-z)\,K(x,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(w'-z)\,K(y,y,z)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
where $K(x,y,z) = \frac{(x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}$
\section{JIMWLK contribution}
\begin{align}

& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\Big[\notag\\
& -2\,N_{c}\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& -2\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x) +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(x)\,U^{hl}(w')\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\notag\\
& +2\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{eb}(w')\,U^{hl}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\Big[\notag\\

\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\mathrm{NLO}}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{1}{N^{2}_{c}-1}\,{\color{blue}\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}}\,\int_{x,y,z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\langle\rho^{f}(x)\rho^{f}(y) \rangle\,\Bigg[\notag \\ & \mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(w,w',z)\Big[4 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(w')\}\notag\\

&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(w')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w')\}\notag\\

&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(w,w,z)\Big[-2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(w')\}+2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(y)\}\notag\\
&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(w)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(w)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(w)\}\notag\\

&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(x,x,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(w')\}-2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w')U(x)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\,K(y,y,z)\Big[2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)U(y)\}-2 N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(y)\}\notag\\
&+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^eU^\dagger(z)U(y)\}\notag\\
&-{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)\}\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(y)U(z)\}\Big]\Bigg]
\end{align}
where $\mathcal{K}(x,y,z) = \frac{(x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}$
\section{DGLAP contribution}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm DGLAP}}{d^{3}k}= &-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\frac{g^{2}}{8\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{\xi^{2}}\,\widetilde{\mathcal P}_{gg}(\xi)\,\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')\frac{1}{\xi}}\Big[2\gamma_{E}+\log\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4\,\xi^{2}}\Big]\frac{(x-y)\cdot(x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}(x'-y')^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\left[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\right]\left[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\right]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)
\end{align}
where:
$\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{gg}(\xi)$ is gluon-gluon splitting function.
$$\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{gg}(\xi) = 2\,N_{c}\left[\xi(1-\xi) + \frac{\xi}{(1-\xi)_{+}} + \Big[\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\Big]_{+}\right]+\frac{\beta_{0}}{2}\,\delta(1-\xi),$$
\section{Running coupling contribution}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm RC}}{d^{3}k}= & +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\frac{g^{2}\beta_{0}}{16\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')}\Big[2\gamma_{E}+\log\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\Big]\frac{(x-y)\cdot(x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}(x'-y')^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\left[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\right]\left[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\right]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)
\end{align}
We take $\frac{g^{2}\beta_{0}}{16\pi^{2}}=\frac{\alpha_{s}\beta_{0}}{4\pi}$ with $\alpha_{s}=\frac{g^{2}}{4\pi}$, and since $\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm RC}}{d^{3}k}$ has exactly the prefactor and the integrand of the LO cross section, the sum of LO and RC is
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm LO}}{d^{3}k}+\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm RC}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{2}(\mu)}{2\pi^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')}\left[1+\frac{\alpha_{s}(\mu)\,\beta_{0}}{4\pi}\log\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\,\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}\,e^{2\gamma_{E}}}{4}\right]\frac{(x-y)\cdot(x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\left[U^{ce'}(y')-U^{ce'}(x')\right]\left[U^{ce}(y)-U^{ce}(x)\right]\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x).
\end{align}
\section{Finite terms}
Finite terms are obtained by $\Lambda \to 0$ and $\vee\to \infty $ and also by considering $d_{\perp} = 2-\epsilon$ and then we take $\epsilon \to 0$. 
\notebox{The conventions of the verified file (Sec.~``Conventions'': phase $e^{-ik\cdot({\rm unprimed}-{\rm primed})}$, c.c.\ in Groups I and III, the plus prescriptions $\int_0^{k^+}dp^+[\frac1{p^+}]_+f(p^+)=\int_0^{k^+}\frac{dp^+}{p^+}[f(p^+)-f(0)]$, $[\frac{k^+}{k^+-p^+}]_+$ subtracting at $p^+=k^+$, $[\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}]_+$ at $p^+=0$, each acting on the whole $p^+$-dependent function of the term (recoil phase and energy denominators); the instantaneous terms $\int_0^\infty dp^+e^{-isp^+/k^+}=k^+[\pi\delta(s)-i\mathcal P\frac1s]$ with the two terms of their bracket under one $y'$ integral; upper limits $k^+$ (Group I) and $\infty$ (Group III; $\vee\to\infty$ before $\epsilon\to0$); PV for the vanishing energy denominators of $\mathbb B_3$ plus the $i\epsilon$ term $\Delta N$; pure glue) were deleted. They are needed to read the formulas below unambiguously. Please restore them.}

\subsection*{Three $\rho$ terms}
\subsection{Group I (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{2b}}{d^{3}k} & = -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{hbc}\,U^{ah}(w)\left\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\right\}-f^{abc}\,U^{bd}(x)\,U^{ce}(y)\left\{\rho^{d}(x),\rho^{e}(y)\right\}\Big]\,,\label{eq:B-I2b}\\
R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p}) & = -2q^{i}\log\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{\mathbf{q}^{2}}\right)+\frac{(2\mathbf{p}^{2}-2\,\mathbf{q}\cdot\mathbf{p}+\mathbf{q}^{2})\,q^{i}+\mathbf{q}^{2}p^{i}}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\,\log\left[\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\right]
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\Bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag \\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\times\, \log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag \\
&+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]\notag \\
&+\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(z)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\ &-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}\notag\\
&\times\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\,U^{de}(x)\Big[U^{bc}(w)-U^{bc}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\label{eq:B-I3}
\end{align}
\notebox{\textbf{(1) Error in the last term of $N_{3b}$:} in the line above, $e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$ has the recoil factor $\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$ \emph{outside} the exponent (a multiplicative factor). The verified term (and the author's own Sec.~6, Eq.~\eqref{eq:D-I.III-3}) is
\[{\color{red}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{4\pi^{4}}\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,[\dots]}\]
(only a brace is misplaced in the \LaTeX\ source, line 183 of the author's file: the brace that closes the exponent stands before \texttt{\textbackslash frac\{p\^{}\{+\}\}\{k\^{}\{+\}\}} instead of after it). As typeset, $\int_0^{k^+}dp^+[\frac1{p^+}]_+\frac{p^+}{k^+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)}=e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)}$ replaces $\int_0^{k^+}dp^+[\frac1{p^+}]_+e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)p^+/k^+}$; the two differ by up to $2.9$ (check (4) of \texttt{s11\_author1\_checks.py}). Verified by TeX comparison with the verified file (all other factors of Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3} identical).}
\notebox{\textbf{(2) Prescription of Row III: principal value only (author's decision, 2026-11-22: ``i dont need +i\epsilon approch for GI, Row III'').} Removing $\Delta N$ is therefore correct. Two consequences: (a) the conventions should state that the energy denominators of $\mathbb{B}_{3}$ (Group I Row III) are taken as principal values, i.e.\ the result is the spectrum of standing-wave (PV) dressed gluons; (b) the commented-out Sec.~7 still contains the averaged $\Delta N$ rows (Eqs.~(E-I.IIIdN-3), (E-I.IIIdN-2)), which refer to the deleted Eq.~(B-DN); they should be deleted before Sec.~7 is uncommented.}

{\color{green}\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.IV}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\times\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag \\ & \times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\Big[f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{c'}(x)-f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{d}(x)\Big]\label{eq:B-I4}
\end{align}}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\times\Big[e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\bigg[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)\,\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(y')\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(y')\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(y')\}\bigg]\,.\label{eq:B-I5}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I (first part)}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{1a}}{d^{3}k} & = -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\Bigg]\times\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\times\,\notag \\
&\Bigg[U^{ac'}(w')\,\{\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\}-U^{c'b}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(y)\,\{\rho^{d'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\}\Bigg]\Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II1a}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{1b}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\Bigg]\times\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\right]\times\,\notag \\
&\Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{d'}(x)-f^{ac'd'}\,U^{c'b}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\Bigg]\Bigg[U^{ac}(w)\,\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\}-U^{cb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\}\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II1b}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Rows II--IV} \textsc{NA}
\subsection{Group III (with c.c.)}

{\color{green}\begin{align}

& -\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Bigg]\times\, \log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\
& \Bigg[U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{d'}(y)-U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{c'}(x')\rho^{e'}(y)\Bigg]\times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-III1}
\end{align}}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k} & = -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}} +\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag \\ & -\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\Bigg]\times\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\times\notag \\
&\Bigg[U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'b}(z)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')\Bigg]\Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-III2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III (first part)}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ac}(w)\,\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{cb}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\Big].\label{eq:B-III3one}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times U^{ce}(x)\Big[U^{ad}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\Big]\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y).\label{eq:B-III5}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row VI}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\Big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\times U^{ad}(y)\Big[U^{d'e}(x)-U^{d'e}(z)\Big]\rho^{d}(y)\rho^{e}(x).\label{eq:B-III6}
\end{align}
\subsection*{Two $\rho$ terms}
\subsection{Group I (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{ab}(w)-U^{ab}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\label{eq:B-I2a}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Rows III, IV} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{\xi\,(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\xi(1-\xi)\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&+(1-\xi)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\xi\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[-f^{adc}\,f^{fbe}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)+N_{c}\,U^{af}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\,,\label{eq:B-I5two}
\end{align}
\notebox{\textbf{(3) $\xi$ is not defined} in this file (the definition was in the deleted explanatory text; in Sec.~3 $\xi$ is the DGLAP fraction, in the verified Group II Row IV $\xi=k^+/(p^++k^+)$). This equation is correct for $\xi\equiv p^{+}/k^{+}$: then $\frac{1}{\xi(y-x)^2+(1-\xi)(y-z)^2}=\frac{k^+}{p^+(y-x)^2+(k^+-p^+)(y-z)^2}$, $\xi(1-\xi)=\frac{p^+(k^+-p^+)}{k^{+2}}$, $1-\xi=\frac{k^+-p^+}{k^+}$, i.e.\ it is the $p^+$ form of Sec.~6, Eq.~\eqref{eq:D-I.V-2o} (check (3): $4\times10^{-16}$; for $\xi=1-p^+/k^+$ it would be wrong). Add ``$\xi\equiv p^{+}/k^{+}$'' or use the $p^+$ form, as in Sec.~6 (author's request of 2026-11-22).}

\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I (second part)}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag \\ & \times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2a}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2b}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2c}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2d}\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\label{eq:B-II2e}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Rows II, III} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm fin}}{d^{3}k}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\Big[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[U^{ce'}(z)-U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\label{eq:B-II4rem}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group III (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Rows I, II} \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row III (second part)}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{p^{+}\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{p^{+}}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag\\ &\times\left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right]\,,\label{eq:B-III3two}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Rows IV--VI} \textsc{NA}
\section{Finite terms: After cancellation and symmetrization}\label{sec:D}
We will use the following relations: 
\begin{align}
C^{abc}\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})&=C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}\Big[f^{abr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}+f^{bcr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{2}-p_{3})\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{r}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}f^{acr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{6}C^{abc}\big[f^{abr}f^{rcs}-f^{bcr}f^{ras}\big]\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\,\rho^{s}(p_{1})_{\rm (iii)}\,,\label{eq:D-ord}\\
C^{abc}\rho^{a}(p_{1})\{\rho^{b}(p_{2}),\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}&=2C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+i\,C^{abc}\Big[f^{abr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{6}C^{abc}\big[f^{abr}f^{rcs}+f^{acr}f^{rbs}\big]\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\,\rho^{s}(p_{1})\,,\label{eq:D-r12}\\
C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}(p_{1}),\rho^{b}(p_{2})\}\rho^{c}(p_{3})&=2C^{abc}\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+i\,C^{abc}\Big[f^{bcr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{2}-p_{3})\{\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{r}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\{\rho^{r}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{6}C^{abc}\big[f^{bcr}f^{ras}+f^{acr}f^{rbs}\big]\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})\,\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})\,\rho^{s}(p_{1})\,.\label{eq:D-r21}
\end{align}
where, $C^{abc}$ is not one specific object. It stands for any $c$-number color tensor multiplying an ordered product of three Wilson lines. 
\notebox{\textbf{(4) Eq.~\eqref{eq:D-ord}:} (a) the second line opens $\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}\big[$ and the third line starts again with $+\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}f^{acr}\dots\big]$, i.e.\ as typeset the third contact term has the factor $(\frac{i}{2}C^{abc})^2$ (and repeated indices). Correct:
\[{\color{red}\begin{aligned}C^{abc}\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})&=C^{abc}\{\rho^a(p_1)\rho^b(p_2)\rho^c(p_3)\}_{\rm sym}+\frac{i}{2}C^{abc}\Big[f^{abr}\delta^{(2)}(p_1-p_2)\{\rho^r(p_1)\rho^c(p_3)\}_{\rm sym}+f^{bcr}\delta^{(2)}(p_2-p_3)\{\rho^a(p_1)\rho^r(p_2)\}_{\rm sym}\\&\quad+f^{acr}\delta^{(2)}(p_1-p_3)\{\rho^r(p_1)\rho^b(p_2)\}_{\rm sym}\Big]-\frac16C^{abc}\big[f^{abr}f^{rcs}-f^{bcr}f^{ras}\big]\delta^{(2)}(p_1-p_2)\delta^{(2)}(p_1-p_3)\rho^s(p_1)\end{aligned}}\]
(checked at the operator level, SU(3) charges on sites, random $C^{abc}$: $4\times10^{-16}$; the typeset form fails by $0.84$ at $p_1=p_3$; the same typo is in our verified file, all rows use the correct form). (b) The subscript ${}_{\rm (iii)}$ on $\rho^{s}(p_{1})$ is a leftover of the deleted \texttt{\textbackslash underbrace}. (c) In the sentence below: ``\dots multiplying an ordered product of three \emph{colour charges} $\rho$'' (not Wilson lines; the Wilson lines are inside $C^{abc}$). (d) The one-$\rho$ terms (the last lines of Eqs.~\eqref{eq:D-ord}--\eqref{eq:D-r21}) are not written in any row below; they average to zero ($\langle\rho^s\rangle=0$), but the rows below equal Sec.~5 at the operator level only with them; say so.}
\notebox{\textbf{(5) Tail-only cancellations.} Only the exact cancellations (Group I Row IV with Group III Row I; the $\frac{11}{3}$ terms of Group I Rows II and V) are applied in this section --- correct. All other terms remain, as they must: the soft remainders of pairs A--D (Group I Rows III, V with Group III Rows III, V, VI), the 24-term kernel with $(\ell-\mathcal E)$ in Row I.III, and Group II Rows I, IV with Group III Row III (joint tail). They are integrable at large distance only in these combinations (pairs under one $z$ integral after $y'\to w'$, pair D also $x\leftrightarrow y$; in Row I.III the $w$ integral before the $z$ integral; the three rows of the joint tail integrated together over the gluon-pair position). This information (deleted Sec.~C of the verified file) must be kept with the result. Missing here: the $\Delta N$ rows (NOTE (2)).}


\subsection{Group I}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]\times \Big[f^{hbc}\,U^{gh}(w)-f^{gde}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ec}(y)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.II-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]\Big[f^{hbc}\,U^{gh}(w)-f^{gde}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ec}(y)\Big]\times\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.II-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{ab}(w)-U^{ab}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x) + {\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.II-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&{-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&{-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&{+\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(z)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{{-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(w)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.III-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&{-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&{-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&{+\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(z)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\times\,\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{{-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(w)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\times\,\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.III-2}
\end{align}
\notebox{\textbf{Dropped:} the $i\epsilon$ rows of Row III (verified ``Row III, $i\epsilon$ prescription ($\eta=+1$): the additional term $\Delta N$'', Eqs.~(D-I.IIIdN-3), (D-I.IIIdN-2)) belong here; see NOTE (2) after Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-I3}.}

\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA} - canceled with Group III, Row I
\subsubsection*{Row V}
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm I.V}}&\equiv \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{gde}\Big[U^{dc}(x)\,U^{eb}(z)-U^{eb}(y)\,U^{dc}(y')\Big]\notag
\end{align}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)}\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-x)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{gde}\Big[U^{dc}(x)\,U^{eb}(z)-U^{eb}(y)\,U^{dc}(y')\Big]\times\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(y')\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.V-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{gde}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)}\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-x)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{dc}(x)\,U^{eb}(z)-U^{eb}(y)\,U^{dc}(y')\Big]\times\,\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y')\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y')\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-I.V-2}
\end{align}
and the genuine two-$\rho$:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\frac{k^{+}\,e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{p^{+}\,(y-x)^{2}+(k^{+}-p^{+})\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}{k^{+\,2}}\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&+\frac{k^{+}-p^{+}}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]}\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]}\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[-f^{adc}\,f^{fbe}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)+N_{c}\,U^{af}(y)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w')}}\,e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\,+ {\rm c.c.}
\label{eq:D-I.V-2o}
\end{align}
\notebox{\textbf{UV (Sec.~\ref{sec:chk-UV}):} this two-$\rho$ term is not absolutely integrable at the collinear point $z\to x$: its kernel is a pure second harmonic $a_2\cos2(\varphi-\varphi_0)/|z-x|^2$ there (the colour factor does not vanish). It is finite only together with its $\frac{11}{6}$ line and with a rotation-invariant treatment of the point $z=x$ (angular average first / dimensional regularisation); without the $\frac{11}{6}$ line it diverges logarithmically. No other term of Sec.~6 needs this.}

\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\times 2\,U^{ga}(w')\notag \\ & \Big[f^{kbc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kb}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]-U^{ga}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\,U^{mb}(y)\Big[f^{khc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\times 2\,U^{gb}(w)\notag \\ & \Big[f^{kca}\,U^{gk}(w')-f^{gkl}\,U^{kc}(z)\,U^{la}(x)\Big]-U^{gb}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\,U^{mc}(y)\Big[f^{kha}\,U^{gk}(w')-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{la}(x)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]\label{eq:D-II.I-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,U^{ga}(w')\Big[f^{kbc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kb}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]-U^{ga}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\,U^{mb}(y)\Big[f^{khc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]\notag \\ & \Big[f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,U^{gb}(w)\Big[f^{kca}\,U^{gk}(w')-f^{gkl}\,U^{kc}(z)\,U^{la}(x)\Big]-U^{gb}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\,U^{mc}(y)\Big[f^{kha}\,U^{gk}(w')-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{la}(x)\Big]\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x')\big\{\rho^{r}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x)\rho^{b}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]\label{eq:D-II.I-2}
\end{align}
Genuine $2-\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag \\ & \times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\notag
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}}{d^{3}k}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot{(y-y')}}\,e^{-ik\cdot{(y-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\Big[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[U^{ce'}(z)-U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)
\label{eq:D-II.IVrem}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group III}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
\textsc{NA} -- Cancels with Group I, Row IV
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\,\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}U^{ma}(y)\,U^{gb}(x')\notag \\ & U^{mh}(z)\,\Big[f^{khc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]-U^{gb}(x')\Big[f^{kac}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{ka}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]\,\big\{\rho^{a}(y)\rho^{b}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.II-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrization:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\,\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
