&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\times\,\notag\\
& \frac{i}{2}\,U^{ma}(y)\,U^{gb}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\Big[f^{khc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]-U^{gb}(x')\Big[f^{kac}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{ka}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x')\big\{\rho^{r}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(y)\rho^{r}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(y)\rho^{b}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.II-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.III-3}
\end{align}
 Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\times i\,f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.III-2}
\end{align}
Genuine $2-\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{p^{+}\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]}\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{p^{+}}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag\\ &\times\left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right]\,+{\rm c.c.}
\label{eq:D-III.III-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{mkl}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.V-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{mkl}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\times\,\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.V-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row VI}
Colour tensors (the row's colour operator is $C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,y'}}\rho^{a}(x')\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{c}(x)$ and the same with the other tensor):
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,y'}}&\equiv f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\notag\\
C^{abc}_{{\rm III.VI,z}}&\equiv f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\notag
\end{align}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{mnk}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.VI-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho,\ {\rm contact}}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{mnk}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\times\,\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\times\,\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.VI-2}
\end{align}
\section{Average over the projectile}\label{sec:E}
Using
\begin{align}
\big\langle\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle=\big\langle\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\big\rangle_{x\neq y}=\delta^{ab}\,\frac{\langle\rho^{f}(x)\rho^{f}(y)\big\rangle}{N_{c}^{2}-1}
\end{align}
the symmetrised three-point function is,
\begin{align}
\big\langle\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle=d^{abc}\,\frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,d^{efg}\{\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{f}(y)\rho^{g}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle
\end{align}

\notebox{\textbf{(8)} In the equation above the opening $\langle$ is missing: correct $\big\langle\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle=d^{abc}\frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\big\langle d^{efg}\{\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{f}(y)\rho^{g}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle$ (coefficient and absence of an $f^{abc}$ part checked: $d^{abc}d^{abc}=(N_c^2-4)(N_c^2-1)/N_c$, $f^{abc}d^{abc}=0$). In the first equation, $\langle\rho^f(x)\rho^f(y)\rangle$ is $n(x,y)$ for $x\neq y$ (the contact parts are in the two-$\rho$ rows). \textbf{Everything after this point in the source (lines 608--945, all averaged Group/Row equations) is inside \texttt{\textbackslash begin\{comment\}\dots\textbackslash end\{comment\}} and is not compiled}: the PDF contains no averaged result. The commented equations are identical to the verified Sec.~E (TeX comparison, all 23 equations), including $\Delta N$ (Eqs.~(E-I.IIIdN-3/-2), which refer to the deleted Eq.~(B-DN)) and the Group II Row IV remnant; they also refer to the deleted Sec.~C1 (would give undefined references when uncommented), and they need the one-line rule $\langle C^{abc}\rho\rho\rho\rangle=\frac{N_c}{(N_c^2-4)(N_c^2-1)}C^{abc}d^{abc}o+\frac{i}{2(N_c^2-1)}[\Phi_{12}\delta_{12}n+\dots]$ of the verified file, which was deleted.}
\begin{comment}
\subsection{Group I (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
four-$\rho$ row, evolution terms only; \textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-2}
\end{align}
Original two-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w')U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{{\color{cPr}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&{\color{pD}+\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{{\color{pA}-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.III-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{{\color{cPr}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{\color{cPr}+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&{\color{pD}+\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{{\color{pA}-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.III-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III, $i\epsilon$ prescription ($\eta=+1$): the additional term $\Delta N$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:B-DN}}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}\Delta N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{-\theta\big(\rho_{0}-|z-w|\big)\,\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\pi\,\delta^{(2)}(z-w)\,\log\frac{\rho_{0}}{|x-w|}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.IIIdN-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}\Delta N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{\eta\,g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{-\theta\big(\rho_{0}-|z-w|\big)\,\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\pi\,\delta^{(2)}(z-w)\,\log\frac{\rho_{0}}{|x-w|}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.IIIdN-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
cancels Group III Row I exactly (Sec.~\ref{sec:C1}); nothing remains
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&{\color{pB}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)}\notag\\
&{\color{pB}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-x)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,o(x',y,y')\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(y)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.V-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&{\color{pB}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)}\notag\\
&{\color{pB}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-x)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,y')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y')\,n(y',y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.V-2}
\end{align}
Original two-$\rho$ terms (second part):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\frac{e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{\xi\,(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\xi(1-\xi)\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&+(1-\xi)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\xi\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&{\color{pC}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]}\notag\\
&{\color{pC}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]}\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\notag\\
&+N_{c}\Big(-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-w')}}\,e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(x-z)}}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\,+{\rm c.c.}\,,\label{eq:E-I.V-2o}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,o(x,x',y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w')\}\Big]\Bigg]\label{eq:E-II.I-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x')\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]\label{eq:E-II.I-2}
\end{align}
Original two-$\rho$ terms (second part):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\notag
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
All terms are quadratic; the DGLAP, RC (Secs.~3, 4) and LO+RC terms in singlet form follow by the replacement $[U^{ce'}(y')-U^{ce'}(x')][U^{ce}(y)-U^{ce}(x)]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\to\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(y)-U(x)\big)\Big\}$; the finite terms are
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm fin}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}}\,e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(y-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(y)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\label{eq:E-II.IV}\\
{\color{red}\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm rem}}{d^{3}k}}=&{\color{red}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)\Big\}}\label{eq:E-II.IVrem}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group III (with c.c.)}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
cancels Group I Row IV exactly (Sec.~\ref{sec:C1}); nothing remains
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(y,x',x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.II-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
& +N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.II-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{\color{pB}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(y')\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.III-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{\color{pB}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.III-2}
\end{align}
Original two-$\rho$ terms (second part):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{\rm singlet} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{p^{+}\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&{\color{pC}-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{\color{pC}\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{p^{+}}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\ &-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-w')}}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\ &-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\,+{\rm c.c.}\,,\label{eq:E-III.III-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{\color{pA}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.V-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{\color{pA}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.V-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row VI}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part (odd in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(3\rho)}_{\rm singlet}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\

&{\color{pD}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\

&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,o(x',y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.VI-3}
\end{align}
Contact (two-$\rho$) part of the three-$\rho$ terms (even in $k$):
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|^{(2\rho)}_{\rm singlet,\ contact}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot\ph{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\

&{\color{pD}-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
& \Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -2{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.VI-2}
\end{align}
\end{comment}


{\color{red}\section*{UV check of every term of Sec.~6 (author's question of 2026-11-22: ``Also check if any term in sec 6 has UV divergence'')}\label{sec:chk-UV}}
\noindent\textbf{Result.} No term of Sec.~6 has an ultraviolet divergence, and no term contains a $1/\epsilon$ pole (the poles were removed by the exact $\frac{11}{3}$ cancellation of Rows I.II/I.V and by the scaleless rule; the remaining kernels are finite functions of the positions). Every symmetrised three-$\rho$ term, every contact (commutator) term and every original two-$\rho$ term is \emph{absolutely} integrable at every coincidence of its integration points (all clusters of 2 to 6 points, including the merged charge points), with one exception: the collinear point $z\to x$ of the two-$\rho$ term of Group I Row V (second part, Eq.~\eqref{eq:D-I.V-2o}), see below. Where the kernel alone is not integrable (only the instantaneous lines of Group III Rows III, V, VI with $x'\to x$: the kernel contains $K(x,x,z)=1/(x-z)^2$, and at $\{x,y',z\}$), the colour factor vanishes at the coincidence identically in the Wilson lines, so the term is integrable for any H\"older-continuous target.

\noindent\textbf{Method} (as \texttt{paper/notes/ref3\_finiteness.tex}; script \texttt{scripts/s10\_uv\_sec6.py}, log \texttt{s10\_uv\_sec6.log}). Kernels as printed in Sec.~6 (the $p^+$ integrals in closed form: $\int_0^{k^+}dp^+[\frac1{p^+}]_+e^{-isp^+/k^+}={\rm Ci}|s|-\gamma_E-\log|s|-i{\rm Si}(s)$ etc.; Row I.V second part and Row III.III second part by quadrature of the printed $p^+$ integrands; the instantaneous terms in the $\delta(s)$ form, $y'$ on the line $s=0$, and in the $\mathcal P\frac1s$ form); colour \emph{at the operator level}: the tensors $C^{abc}$ of Sec.~6 (for the contact terms contracted with $f^{abr}$, $f^{acr}$, $f^{bcr}$, Wilson lines at the merged point; for the two-$\rho$ terms the two-index colour factor), Frobenius norm, with a smooth $SU(3)$ adjoint field $U=\exp(A^af^a)$ (plane waves). For each term and cluster $S$: 3 base configurations $\times$ 10 orientations, $S$ shrunk to $\lambda=10^{-2}\dots10^{-5}$, $|{\rm term}|\sim\lambda^{\alpha}$; absolutely integrable at $S$ iff $\alpha>-d_S$, $d_S=2(|S|-1)$ (one less for $\{y',z\}\subset S$ in the $\delta(s)$ form). Columns: number of clusters; most singular cluster with colour ($S$, $d_S$, $\alpha$); most singular cluster of the kernel alone ($\alpha_0$); verdict; reason (``kernel'': the kernel alone is integrable at every cluster; ``colour $\to0$'': clusters where only the vanishing colour factor makes it integrable). Group I Row II ($N_{2b}$, momentum space): \texttt{scripts/s12\_N2b\_momentum.py}.

\noindent\textbf{Row I.II, $N_{2b}$} (Eqs.~\eqref{eq:D-I.II-3}, \eqref{eq:D-I.II-2}). The kernel $\frac{1}{\mathbf q^2}\frac12[\frac{R^i(\mathbf q,\mathbf p)}{\mathbf p^2}-\frac{R^i(\mathbf q,\mathbf q-\mathbf p)}{(\mathbf q-\mathbf p)^2}]$ is exactly homogeneous of degree $-3$ in $(\mathbf p,\mathbf q)$ (to $10^{-16}$), so the coordinate kernel is homogeneous of degree $-1$ in $(x-w,y-w)$: integrable at $\{x,y,w\}$ ($d=4$). Large $|\mathbf p|$ at fixed $\mathbf q$ ($x\to y$): $\log p/p^3$ (bounded coordinate kernel); large $|\mathbf q|$ at fixed $\mathbf p$ ($y\to w$) and at fixed $\mathbf p-\mathbf q$ ($x\to w$): the leading $\log q/q$ terms of the two parts cancel, $(\log q)^k/q^2$ with $1<k<2$, so at most a power of $\log r$ in coordinates. With $\mathcal K(x'-w')$ (or $\mathcal K(x-w')$, $\mathcal K(y-w')$ after a contact): $r^{-1}$ at $\{x',w'\}$, $r^{-2}$ at $\{x,y,w,x',w'\}$ ($d=8$). The $d^2q$ integral at large $q$ converges as a Fourier integral (oscillatory), the result is integrable. No UV pole (the scaleless $\int d^2p/\mathbf p^2$ is zero by the antisymmetrised form, and the $\frac{11}{3}$ terms cancel exactly against Row V).

\noindent\textbf{The one exception: Group I Row V, second part, at $z\to x$} (Eq.~\eqref{eq:D-I.V-2o}; table rows ``I.V 2nd part''). At $z=x$ the colour factor of the first group of lines, $[U(x')-U(w')]^{ab'}[-f^{adc}f^{fbe}U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)+N_cU^{af}(y)]$, becomes $N_c[U(x')-U(w')]^{ab'}[U(y)-U(x)]^{af}$, i.e.\ $N_c$ times that of the $\frac{11}{6}$ line: it does not vanish. The kernel of the sum behaves as $a_2\cos2(\varphi-\varphi_0)/|z-x|^2$ (pure second harmonic; the angular mean falls like $|z-x|^0$ after the $\frac{11}{6}$ term, i.e.\ the $\frac{1}{|z-x|^2}$ monopole cancels between the regular, soft and $\frac{11}{6}$ lines; without the $\frac{11}{6}$ line the mean is a non-zero constant times $1/|z-x|^2$: logarithmically divergent). So the term is \emph{not} absolutely integrable at $z\to x$ ($\alpha=-2$, $d=2$): it is finite only (i) together with its $\frac{11}{6}$ line and (ii) with a rotation-invariant treatment of the point $z=x$ (angular integration first, or any rotation-invariant regulator such as dimensional regularisation, which is what produced the $\frac{11}{6}$). A non-circular cut-off changes the result by a finite amount $a_2\int d\varphi\cos2(\varphi-\varphi_0)\log R(\varphi)$. Caveat (from \texttt{ref3\_finiteness.tex}): in $d_\perp=2-\epsilon$ the traceless tensor $\hat r^i\hat r^m-\frac12\delta^{im}$ has trace $O(\epsilon)$, which times the $\frac1\epsilon$ of the collinear subtraction could leave a finite local term; this scheme question is not decided here. This is not a UV divergence and not a pole, but the term must be integrated in this way.

\noindent\textbf{Precision and checks of the numbers.} Minimal margin $\alpha+d_S$ over all 2077 clusters of the 64 scanned lines, Row I.V second part excluded: $0.88$ (a $\log(1/r)/r$ at $d=2$, Row I.III, 24-term kernel; the same kernel at $\{z,w\}$ was rechecked with 60 digits in \texttt{paper/notes/scripts/ref3\_finite/mp\_Mij.py}: $\log(1/r)/r$). The kernel-alone exponent $\alpha_0=-3.16$ of Row II.I (second part) at $\{z,w,w'\}$ is rounding loss in the cancellation of the $r^{-4}$ terms of $N_{2a},\dots,N_{2e}$: with 60 digits (\texttt{scripts/s14\_mp\_II1.py}) $|N_{2a}+\dots+N_{2e}|\simeq0.76\lambda^{-2}$ for $\lambda=10^{-3}\dots10^{-9}$, integrable ($d=4$). Row I.V second part at $z\to x$, operator-level colour (\texttt{scripts/s13\_V2\_collinear.py}): $\lambda^2|\text{angular mean}|=1.8\times10^{-4},1.8\times10^{-6},1.8\times10^{-8}$ for $\lambda=10^{-2},10^{-3},10^{-4}$ (mean bounded), $\lambda^2\max=0.35,0.34,0.34$ (second harmonic $\propto1/\lambda^2$); without the $\frac{11}{6}$ line $\lambda^2|\text{mean}|=3.03$ (constant: $\frac{1}{|z-x|^2}$ monopole, log divergent). The previous power counting of the contact terms in singlet form (\texttt{paper/notes/ref3\_finiteness.tex}, 1222 clusters) agrees with the operator-level results here.
